【茅臺集團旗下子公司人事變動，茅臺保健酒、茅臺葡萄酒迎新任董事長】茅臺集團旗下子公司近期出現人事變動。紅星資本局獲悉，李春風已出任貴州茅臺酒廠（集團）保健酒業有限公司（以下簡稱“茅臺保健酒公司”）董事長並擔任法定代表人，張迪已出任貴州茅臺酒廠（集團）昌黎葡萄酒業有限公司（以下簡稱“茅臺葡萄酒公司”）董事長。公開資訊顯示，李春風早期在茅臺集團內部任職，曾任‌貴州茅臺酒廠智慧財產權保護部辦公室主任‌、茅臺集團黨委辦公室副主任等職務。2023年4月，正式接任茅臺葡萄酒公司法定代表人、董事長，全面主持茅臺葡萄酒板塊工作。張迪曾履職茅臺酒區域自營銷售公司，負責區域市場渠道運營管理，積累銷售經驗後，出任貴州茅臺酒廠（集團）迴圈經濟產業投資開發有限公司黨委副書記、總經理。(紅星資本局)

### 2026-10-15 11:35:07  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #SSE Composite #Impact bearish #SZSE Component

【China stocks midday: Shanghai Composite down 1.98%; media sector rebounds】After a weak open, major Chinese indexes slid further; the Shanghai Composite breached 3,700 intraday and closed the morning session down 1.98%. The Shenzhen Composite Index fell 2.78% and the ChiNext index dropped 3.42%. Media names staged a recovery, with Neimeng Xinhua and Duzhe Media hitting daily limit-ups. Baijiu stocks spiked then pulled back; Huangtai Distillery recorded two daily limit-ups in three sessions. Brokers were volatile—Hualin Securities surged over 7% intraday. Component suppliers, innovative drugs, CROs and traditional Chinese medicine sectors were active. Shipping and ports led declines, with COSCO Shipping Energy and China Merchants Nanyou among several stocks hitting limit-downs. Other notable laggards included crude palm oil, semiconductors, coal, grains, aerospace equipment and oil & petrochemicals. Combined turnover on the Shanghai and Shenzhen markets through midday was about 981.8 bln yuan, roughly 129.0 bln yuan higher than the same period yesterday; roughly 400 stocks advanced and about 4,700 declined.

### 2026-10-15 11:31:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Soybeans Fall to 7-Week Low — Soybean futures fell to around $12.7 per bushel, hitting their lowest level in seven weeks as active US harvesting boosted supply prospects, despite renewed Chinese buying. Mostly dry weather across the Midwest over the past week allowed farmers to accelerate harvesting after earlier rain delays, with 46% of the US crop harvested as of Sunday, above the average estimate for 44% complete. Although midweek rains slowed fieldwork this week, drier conditions are forecast in the coming days. Meanwhile, Chinese government buyers booked 8 to 10 cargoes of US soybeans for shipment from December onward, and the USDA confirmed private sales of 470,000 tonnes to China in a daily export sales announcement on Wednesday. Traders are now awaiting September US crush data from NOPA, with analysts expecting processing to fall to a one-year low of 203.737 million bushels. End-of-month soyoil stocks are also projected at 1.116 billion pounds, the lowest since November 2024.

【午評：雙創指數半日均跌超3%，全市場超5000只個股下跌】A股三大指數早盤集體下跌，截至午盤，上證指數跌1.98%，深證成指跌2.78%，創業板指跌3.42%，北證50跌2.76%，科創50指數跌3.02%。全市場成交額9896億元，較上日放量1289億元，全市場超5000只個股下跌。板塊題材上，煤炭開採加工、油氣開採及服務、港口航運、半導體、豬肉、種植業與林業板塊跌幅居前；白酒、元件、醫療服務、證券板塊表現相對領先。盤面上，美國公佈的最新通脹資料超預期，加息機率上升，對利率敏感的科技板塊全線回撥，長鑫科技跌超4%總市值跌破3萬億元，總市值退居至工商銀行之後，“易中天”同樣表現不佳推動指數震盪走弱。中東局勢急劇惡化，兩艘新加坡籍貨輪遇襲，航運板塊大幅調整，中遠海能、招商南油、招商輪船盤中跌停。煤炭板塊同樣表現落後，昊華能源、兗礦能源、鄭州煤電跌幅居前。此外，豬肉、種植業、半導體等多個板塊下挫。另一方面，白酒板塊逆勢上漲，皇臺酒業漲停，金種子酒、金徽酒跟漲，分析人士指出資金尋找“避風港”，流向具備充沛現金流、低估值和高分紅特徵的大消費板塊。科技板塊中的PCB概念股表現堅挺，澳弘電子19天11板，駿亞科技、金祿電子等股漲停，此前多家PCB公司公佈的業績預告亮眼。

【不放假！11月16日至20日，深圳倡議企業靈活工作安排】10月15日，深圳市政府新聞辦舉行APEC會議“綠色出行、暢通有我”主題新聞釋出會。釋出會上，深圳市人力資源保障局副局長郝時飛介紹，深圳倡導企業根據生產、工作的具體情況及所在區域，在APEC會議當週工作日期間（11月16日至20日）實行靈活工作安排。對此，有記者提問關於倡導企業實行靈活工作安排，企業應具體如何響應實施？深圳市人力資源保障局郝時飛表示，會議期間的交通秩序、城市執行綜合服務保障要求高、涉及面廣，離不開廣大企業職工的共同參與，同心攜手。靈活工作安排包括居家遠端辦公、錯峰上下班、彈性上下班、休帶薪年休假等多種方式。企業可在與職工充分協商的基礎上，結合實際因企施策、因崗施策、因時施策、因地施策，不同企業、同一企業的不同崗位，均可選擇不同的方式。(南方plus)

### 2026-10-15 11:24:55  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Gas Prices Stay Elevated — European natural gas prices hovered near €87 per megawatt-hour on Thursday, their highest level since 2022, amid Persian Gulf supply risks and low winter storage reserves. Shipping disruptions through the Strait of Hormuz have constrained LNG flows from the Middle East, which accounts for around 20% of global LNG exports. The loss of Gulf supplies has intensified competition for spot cargoes, tightening availability for Europe and complicating efforts to replenish inventories. European gas storage facilities are currently around 74% full, below historical averages for this time of year. Storage replenishment could face further challenges from early-season cold weather, with updated forecasts pointing to a significant cold snap across northeastern Europe from next weekend that could boost heating demand. Meanwhile, the recovery in Norwegian pipeline supply provided some relief.

### 2026-10-15 11:24:45  #Trading Economics #Markets #Philippines #Currency #Importance 1

Philippine Peso Slides to Record Low — The Philippine peso depreciated to around 63 per dollar in mid-October, hitting a record low as concerns over the country's economic outlook worsened. The IMF warned that the Philippines could record the fifth-slowest economic growth in Southeast Asia this year, projecting GDP growth of 3.4%, down from 4.4% in 2025, as weak investment and elevated inflation weigh on economic activity. Growth has remained subdued, with the energy crisis triggered by the Middle East war adding to the lingering fallout from the flood control scandal. The IMF also projected the jobless rate to ease to 5.2% by 2027, but remain above its previous estimate of 4.6%. Continued depreciation of the Philippine peso has intensified calls for central bank intervention, with Michael Ricafort, chief economist at Rizal Commercial Banking Corp., noting that additional interest rate hikes are becoming more likely to help stabilize the currency.

Offshore Yuan Lingers Near 2022 High — The offshore yuan strengthened to around 6.69 per dollar on Thursday, lingering near its strongest level since July 2022 even as the IMF said China’s stimulus efforts were insufficient and flagged the currency’s undervaluation. Beijing has introduced mortgage subsidies and expanded central bank support for targeted sectors, while the State Council pledged to roll out “a package of practical and effective additional policies” to meet this year’s economic goals. However, the IMF has called for more ambitious measures, estimating that the yuan is undervalued in real terms by 17.3% to 25.3% in 2026, with a midpoint of 21.3%. China’s currency valuation has become a flashpoint in global trade discussions, with debate centering on whether it drives trade imbalances or reflects broader economic conditions. The PBoC argued that the IMF’s assessment does not constitute a judgment on the yuan’s nominal exchange rate or justify calls for a deliberate appreciation against specific currencies.

Euro Rebounds — The euro rebounded to around $1.12 from its lowest level in a year and a half, as the US dollar retreated after Federal Reserve officials signaled they could wait for a clearer picture before taking further action. The cautious remarks prompted traders to scale back bets on an October rate hike, offsetting support for the greenback from hotter-than-expected US inflation data. Meanwhile, expectations that the ECB would postpone further rate hikes until December, coupled with concerns that persistent inflation could dampen economic growth, continued to pressure the euro. Fiscal uncertainty in highly indebted countries, particularly France, further clouded the currency's outlook. ECB President Christine Lagarde said energy-driven inflation had yet to spill over into the broader euro-area economy, supporting a cautious approach to monetary policy. However, ECB policymaker Gediminas Šimkus maintained that a rate hike this month remained possible if needed.

Peru Trade Surplus Hits Record High in August — Peru’s trade surplus widened to a record high of USD 4,714 million in August 2026, up from USD 1,421 million in the same month a year earlier. Exports surged 36.9% year-on-year to USD 10,815 million, driven by higher shipments of traditional products (46.9%), non-traditional products (7.2%), and other products (58.4%). Meanwhile, imports increased 18.8% to USD 6,101 million, led by gains in consumer goods (21.1%), supplies (27.1%), and capital goods (13.4%), while imports of other goods fell 96.0%. Over the first eight months of the year, Peru recorded a trade surplus of USD 30,793 million, up sharply from USD 18,503 million in the same period last year. Exports totaled USD 77,400 million, 37.1% higher than a year earlier, while imports amounted to USD 46,607 million, an increase of 22.8%.

RBNZ Signals Flexibility Amid Inflation Pressures — Reserve Bank of New Zealand Chief Economist Paul Conway said Thursday that the central bank’s late-October rate decision remains open despite mounting price pressures. Speaking at IMF and World Bank meetings in Bangkok, he stressed that policymakers want flexibility to either hold the Official Cash Rate or raise it by 25bps. While a hike is still seen as the most likely outcome, its timing is uncertain. Conway noted oil price gains and a weaker New Zealand dollar have added near-term inflation pressure, complicating the outlook. Still, the board expects inflation to ease back into its 1–3% target next year. With risks skewed to higher near-term inflation, officials are keeping both options open, with the scale and timing of any tightening dependent on incoming data.

### 2026-10-15 10:58:24  #市場 #央行 #國際

【美聯儲“褐皮書”顯示高油價對經濟產生廣泛影響】美國聯邦儲備委員會14日釋出的全國經濟形勢調查報告顯示，8月25日到10月5日的最近一個報告期內，高油價讓美國面臨廣泛的物價上漲壓力，並對經濟產生廣泛影響。 根據美國“聯邦儲備法”，美國全國劃分為12個聯邦儲備區，每區設立一家聯邦儲備銀行。該報告根據12家聯邦儲備銀行的最新調查結果編制而成，也稱“褐皮書”。 報告顯示，3個聯邦儲備區整體物價強勁上漲，8個聯邦儲備區物價溫和上漲，僅1個聯邦儲備區物價漲幅較小。此外，有4個聯邦儲備區報告，物價增速自前一報告期以來已經加快。油價上漲影響到所有聯邦儲備區。企業普遍表示柴油價格處於高位，成本上漲透過燃油附加費向整個供應鏈傳導。 報告說，多數聯邦儲備區的企業對未來幾個月的經濟前景保持樂觀，但其預期受到持續不確定性的拖累。 美聯儲每年釋出8次“褐皮書”，透過聯邦儲備銀行對全美經濟形勢進行摸底。該報告是美聯儲貨幣政策會議的重要參考資料。美聯儲下次貨幣政策會議將於10月27日至28日舉行。(新華社)

### 2026-10-15 10:48:12  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

China 10Y Yield at Over 1-Year Low — China’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 1.65% on Thursday, hitting its lowest level since May 2025, as a strengthening yuan and lackluster economy bolstered demand for Chinese sovereign debt. The yield has declined 18 basis points since the end of December, while Chinese bonds have delivered nearly 10% returns over the past 12 months for dollar-based investors. Chinese bonds have also benefited from the country’s relative insulation from rising oil prices, supported by substantial strategic reserves and the continued shift toward electrified transport. Beijing’s efforts to attract more foreign investors to its domestic bond market are providing another source of support as China seeks to expand the yuan’s international role. The rally has widened the yield gap between Chinese and US government bonds to a record this month, with China’s 10-year yield at 1.67%, 352 basis points below that of comparable-maturity US Treasuries.

Indonesian Equities Under Strain — Indonesia’s IDX Composite slipped 66 points, or 1.1%, to 5,876 in early Thursday trade, reversing the prior session’s modest gain as sentiment soured amid a global equity selloff. Chip stocks plunged, and faster-than-expected U.S. inflation stoked concern over further rate hikes, weighing on Asian markets. Locally, caution intensified ahead of MSCI’s November review of Indonesian equities, while the bourse added 19 names to its High Shareholding Concentration list. Still, losses were partly cushioned by the IMF’s decision to raise Indonesia’s 2026 growth forecast to 5.1% from 5.0%, citing support for household purchasing power and stronger government spending toward year-end. All sectors were in the red, led by energy, basic materials, transport, and property. Notable decliners included Indokripto Koin Semesta (-10.0%), Gojek Tokopedia (-7.1%), Mitra Adiperkasa (-6.4%), and Impack Pratama Industri (-5.5%).

Dollar Weakens as Markets Assess Fed Outlook — The US dollar index slipped to around 102 on Thursday, retreating from 18-month highs as investors continued to evaluate the Federal Reserve’s monetary policy outlook following stronger-than-expected inflation data. Figures released Wednesday showed that annual headline inflation accelerated to 3.7% in September, surpassing forecasts of 3.6%, while core inflation edged up to 2.6%, above expectations of 2.5%. Chicago Fed's Austan Goolsbee described the figures as “discouraging” and cautioned that the rapid expansion of AI infrastructure could also be overheating the economy. However, some policymakers continued to favor a cautious approach to further rate increases, with markets pricing in roughly a 73% probability that the Fed will keep interest rates unchanged this month. Investors are now awaiting further remarks from Fed Chair Kevin Warsh ahead of the central bank’s media blackout, which begins on Saturday. The dollar weakened the most against the euro, sterling and kiwi.

Philippines Stocks Hit Lowest Since 2020 — The Philippine Stock Exchange index (PSEi) continued its downward trend below 5,600 on Thursday, its lowest level since May 2020, extending its year-to-date losses to around 8% as concerns over the country's economic outlook deepened. Jordan Industrial Output Hits 18-Month High — Industrial output in Jordan climbed 3.3% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from a 1.2% increase in the previous month and marking the highest reading since February 2025. Manufacturing output, the largest component of the index, accounting for 88.7% of total industrial activity, rose to an 18-month high of 3.7% year-on-year compared with 2.0% in July. At the same time, growth in electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply accelerated to 3.5% from a flat reading previously, while the decline in mining and quarrying production softened (-3.0% vs -9.7%). On a monthly basis, industrial production increased 3.2%, easing from 4.6% in the previous month. For the January-August 2026 period, industrial output rose 0.6% compared with the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-10-15 10:33:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Remittances #Importance 1

Philippine Cash Remittances Up 1.9% in August — Cash remittances coursed through banks in the Philippines rose by 1.9% year-on-year to USD 3.03 billion in August 2026, driven by higher receipts from both land-based (2.2%) and sea-based (0.8%) sources. In the first eight months of the year, cash remittances increased by 2.2% to USD 23.42 billion, with inflows from land-based and sea-based workers rising by 2.3% and 1.9%, respectively. During this period, the US remained the largest source of inflows, accounting for 39.8% of total remittances, followed by Singapore (7.1%), Saudi Arabia (6.3%), Japan (4.9%), and the UK (4.8%). Meanwhile, seasonally adjusted personal remittances, which include transfers through banks and informal channels, as well as in-kind transfers, increased by 2.0% to USD 3.37 billion in August and rose by 2.3% to USD 26.10 billion in the January-August period.

US 10-Year Treasury Yield Drops to 3-Week Low — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note fell to around 5.11% on Thursday, hitting a three-week low and marking a sharp reversal after reaching as high as 5.33% in the previous session following the release of strong inflation data. The decline came as a deepening selloff in equity markets, led by technology and artificial intelligence stocks, drove investors toward safe-haven government bonds. Data released Wednesday showed that annual headline inflation accelerated to 3.7% in September, exceeding forecasts of 3.6%, while core inflation edged up to 2.6%, above expectations of 2.5%. Chicago Fed's Austan Goolsbee described the figures as “discouraging” and warned that the rapid expansion of AI-related infrastructure could also be overheating the economy. However, some policymakers continued to advocate a cautious approach toward further rate increases, with markets currently pricing in roughly a 73% probability that the Federal Reserve will leave interest rates unchanged this month.

TAIEX Trades at 4-Week Low as Tech Sector Weighs — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, shed 1,427 points, or 2.9%, to 47,199 in Thursday morning trade, after gaining in the previous session, touching its lowest level since September 18. The broader index tracked a decline on Wall Street overnight amid losses in the tech sector, as higher-than-expected inflation data raised expectations of a Fed rate hike. Rising oil prices also pressured sentiment amid concerns over inflation and interest rate hikes. Most sectors traded in the red, led by electronic technology, energy minerals, technology services, and process industries. Electronic technology fell 3.2%, tracking the tech decline on Wall Street, with TSMC, the world’s largest contract chipmaker, which accounts for more than 40% of the market’s total value, falling 1.8%. Meanwhile, MediaTek slumped 5.%, Delta Electronics plunged 6.7%, while Hon Hai Precision Industry dropped 1.9%.

【我國科學家發現石墨烯膜“層間摺疊卡扣”結構】記者15日從浙江大學獲悉，我國科學家首次在石墨烯膜中發現“層間摺疊卡扣”新結構與新機制，研究可實現材料強度、韌性和彈性的協同提升。相關成果於北京時間14日23時線上發表於國際學術期刊《自然》。長期以來，石墨烯膜的力學效能提升面臨“強度與韌性難以兼顧”瓶頸，傳統方法或透過提高石墨烯片層排列的規整程度，或增強片層之間的結合力來改善效能。新研究突破了傳統“平面堆疊”設計思路，構築出層間相互咬合的摺疊卡扣結構石墨烯膜。論文作者之一、浙江大學高超教授說，該研究有望為航空航天輕量化結構、抗衝擊防護裝備等領域的發展提供材料基礎。（新華社）

### 2026-10-15 10:12:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Interest Rate #Importance 1

BoJ Koeda Backs Further Rate Hikes Amid Inflation Risks — The Bank of Japan (BoJ) should not be overly cautious about further rate hikes given persistent upside price risks and still-accommodative financial conditions, board member Junko Koeda said in a speech. She called for timely policy adjustments based on economic and price developments, while considering the estimated range of the neutral interest rate. Koeda noted that price increases across a broad range of consumer items and a shift in the distribution of price changes suggest underlying inflation is becoming anchored around 2%. Stable wage growth also indicates that sustained inflation is taking hold more firmly in Japan. She stressed the need to deepen the BoJ’s analysis of economic conditions, prices, and financial markets as positive interest rates become a normal feature of economic activity.

### 2026-10-15 10:05:46  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Currency #Importance 1

Kiwi Dollar Edges Higher but Remains Near 2025 Lows — The New Zealand dollar rose to $0.561 on Thursday, having fallen to as low as $0.556 overnight, its weakest level since April 2025, after higher-than-expected US CPI data boosted the greenback. The report prompted traders to increase bets on another Federal Reserve rate hike later this month and a further increase in December. Domestically, while the Reserve Bank of New Zealand has resumed its monetary tightening cycle, the official cash rate remains notably lower than interest rates in several major economies, including the US and neighboring Australia, making the kiwi less attractive to yield-seeking investors. The currency has also been weighed down by a still-sluggish economy and ongoing political uncertainty. Investors will now focus on New Zealand’s third-quarter CPI report next week for clues on the interest rate outlook. The report will be the last major data release before the RBNZ’s next meeting on October 28.

China Stocks Hit Over 1-Year Low — The Shanghai Composite fell 1.4% to 3,715 on Thursday, while the Shenzhen Component dropped 1.9% to 11,874, with both indices hitting their lowest levels since August 2025 as investors weighed the potential for renewed trade tensions between China and the EU. Brussels is already considering trade measures targeting other industries, including furniture and scooters, while Germany is expected to push for restrictions on chemicals, mechanical engineering products and potentially pharmaceuticals, following last week’s agreement to limit Chinese car exports. However, China viewed the recent agreement as a calculated and one-off concession, while the Chinese state media also warned of a response if the EU introduces new measures. Energy stocks lagged, led by PetroChina (-1.3%) and CNOOC (-1.5%). Technology stocks also fell, including Cambricon Technologies (-4.6%), Zhongji Innolight (-4.1%), Eoptolink Technology (-4.8%) and NAURA Technology (-3.3%).

Hong Kong Stocks Face Pressure as AI Selloff Deepens — The Hang Seng Index slipped 1.2%, or 290 points, to around 24,030 on Thursday, as hotter-than-expected US inflation data heightened concerns over further Federal Reserve rate hikes, weighing on global technology and AI-related shares. The weakness followed another sharp decline on Wall Street, where the Nasdaq fell 1.88% on Wednesday, posting its biggest one-day drop since July. In the region, technology and semiconductor shares remained under pressure, with investors awaiting TSMC's earnings for further signals on semiconductor demand. Meanwhile, Brent crude eased to around US$104.67 a barrel, although tensions near the Strait of Hormuz continued to raise concerns over energy supplies and inflation. Investors also watched Transsion Holdings' Hong Kong debut, as the smartphone maker sought to raise up to HK$3.36 billion amid rising memory costs and margin pressures. Notable laggards included Tencent (-1.8%), Lenovo (-8.8%), Kingboard Laminates (-5.8%), Z.AI (-6.3%) and SMIC (-4.0%).

### 2026-10-15 10:02:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sri Lanka #Tourist Arrivals #Importance 1

Sri Lanka Tourist Arrivals Extend Fall in September — The number of foreign tourist arrivals in Sri Lanka fell by 0.26% year-on-year to 158,557 in September 2026, following a 3.3% decline in the previous month. This marked the sixth year-on-year monthly decline so far this year, although cumulative tourist arrivals for the first nine months of 2026 reached 1,693,679. India continued to dominate as the top source market, accounting for 32.0% of arrivals with 51,931 visitors, followed by China with 11,722 (7.0%) and Australia with 10,951 (7.0%). The UK ranked fourth with 10,738 visitors, followed by Germany with 7,277. Despite the recent weakness, total arrivals remained 6.4% above the same period in 2018, before the pandemic disrupted international travel. Meanwhile, Sri Lanka cut its 2026 tourist arrival target to 2.7 million from 3 million and lowered its tourism revenue goal to $4.2 billion from $5 billion amid weakening visitor numbers.

### 2026-10-15 09:56:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

AUS 10Y Yield Falls on Soft Jobs Report — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 5.2%, hitting a five-week low after a surprisingly soft jobs report prompted investors to pare back wagers on further rate hikes. The economy shed 39,300 jobs, the biggest monthly fall since February last year, defying expectations for a 20,000 gain. The jobless rate also stayed at a five-year high of 4.7%, above the 4.6% forecast, with the previous month revised up from an initial 4.6% reading. The figures suggested the labor market may be loosening after the Reserve Bank of Australia raised its cash rate by 1 percentage point to 4.60% this year to rein in inflation, one of the most aggressive tightening cycles among developed economies. Markets now see less than a 10% chance of a November hike, down from 35% earlier, while the odds of a December move fell to 30% from 60%. A May 2027 hike carries the highest probability at 80%. Attention now turns to Q3 CPI data due later this month for further clues on the policy outlook.

Silver Steadies as Oil Prices Retreat — Silver steadied above $56 per ounce on Thursday after a sharp fall in the previous session, as crude oil ended its five-day rally, easing pressure across other markets. A softer dollar and retreating bond yields also provided support to metals. However, expectations of more aggressive Federal Reserve rate hikes continued to weigh on sentiment following hotter-than-expected US inflation data. The US Consumer Price Index rose to 3.7% in September, its highest level in four months and above market forecasts of 3.6%, largely driven by rising energy costs. Federal Reserve Bank of Chicago President Austan Goolsbee described the figures as “discouraging” and cautioned that the rapid expansion of artificial intelligence infrastructure could also be overheating the economy. Meanwhile, San Francisco Fed President Mary Daly indicated that the central bank may need to raise short-term interest rates further, although she saw no immediate need for a rate hike at this month’s meeting.

### 2026-10-15 09:21:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Falls to Two-Week Low — The Australian dollar fell below $0.70, hitting a two-week low after higher unemployment and unexpected job cuts in September prompted traders to scale back bets on further rate hikes. The jobless rate came in at 4.7%, above the 4.6% forecast, while the previous month's reading was revised up from an initial 4.6%. The economy also shed 39,300 jobs, compared with expectations for a 20,000 gain, although full-time employment climbed by 34,400. The figures suggested the labor market may be loosening after the Reserve Bank of Australia raised its cash rate by 1 percentage point to 4.60% this year to rein in inflation, marking one of the most aggressive tightening cycles among developed economies. Markets now see less than a 20% chance of a November hike, down from 35% earlier in the day, while the odds of a December move fell to 40% from 60%. Rate markets have also pushed back expectations for further tightening, with May 2027 carrying the highest implied probability of a hike at 80%.

今日12大單品的終端零售總價重現回落，七款產品價格下跌，下跌端量能對整體價盤形成較大拖累。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10745元，較昨日明顯下跌19元。

### 2026-10-15 09:08:13  #其他

【重新整理世界紀錄！我國量子網路領域實現關鍵技術突破】記者14日從中國科學技術大學獲悉，該校潘建偉、張強等與濟南量子技術研究院鄭名揚等組成的研究團隊，在紫外波段離子阱儲存器和通訊光纖的高效量子互聯方面取得重要進展。他們成功實現393奈米至1550奈米的高效率、低噪聲量子頻率轉換，重新整理該波段量子頻率轉換效率與噪聲水平的國際紀錄，為可擴充套件量子網路中囚禁離子節點的遠端互連提供了關鍵技術。相關成果發表在學術期刊《物理評論快報》上。基於以上創新方法，團隊構建393奈米至1550奈米的高效率低噪聲量子頻率轉換模組，端到端效率達28.8%，泵浦光噪聲抑制至35個/秒。與此前研究工作相比，效率提升約30倍，噪聲降低逾兩個數量級，創造了該波段量子頻率轉換的新紀錄。研究人員介紹，該量子頻率轉換模組已在基於鈣離子囚禁離子的遠距離量子糾纏中得到實際應用，訊雜比可達120∶1，支撐了長壽命遠端離子—離子高保真度糾纏的實驗演示與器件無關量子金鑰分發，為可擴充套件量子中繼和廣域量子網路奠定了關鍵器件基礎。

【前特斯拉高管掌舵，引領Uber貨運業務轉型】Rebecca Tinucci 出任 Uber Technologies 旗下物流部門的負責人，目標是讓這項貨運業務完美融入這家全球頂尖的科技巨頭。上任一年後，儘管該部門仍處於虧損狀態，但她的領導已初見成效。今年8月，Uber Freight 報告了三年來的首次連續兩個季度實現年度營收增長。儘管全行業需求疲軟，但透過其網路處理的貨運量依然有所增加。此外，Tinucci 表示，越來越多的客戶正將更大比例的物流需求交由該公司處理。作為 Uber Freight 的執行長，她在首次接受採訪時表示：“如今，貨主們更願意、也更傾向於與更少的供應商合作，以簡化他們的供應鏈。”

【China reports record quantum frequency conversion enabling trapped‑ion telecom links】Researchers at the University of Science and Technology of China and the Jinan Quantum Technology Research Institute reported a high‑efficiency, low‑noise quantum frequency conversion from 393 nm (UV) to 1,550 nm (telecom) that enables interfacing trapped‑ion memories with optical fiber. The team led by Pan Jianwei, Zhang Qiang and Zheng Mingyang said the result sets an international record for conversion efficiency and noise in this wavelength range and provides key technology for remote interconnection of trapped‑ion nodes in scalable quantum networks. The findings are published in Physical Review Letters.

【中信期貨：白銀短線震盪偏弱執行，關注本週美國PPI、零售資料和沃什講話】日內海內外白銀大幅下跌，其中COMEX白銀跌幅超5%、昨夜一度跌至56美元/盎司附近，受美元指數走強及美債收益率高位壓制。美國9月核心CPI同、環比均高於市場預期，表明通脹黏性仍存，推動市場上修美聯儲10月加息機率，美元及美債收益率同步上行，對白銀金融屬性形成進一步壓制。地緣方面，胡塞武裝襲擊沙特能源設施及霍爾木茲海峽周邊航運風險仍存，能源供應不確定性支撐油價高位執行，可能進一步增加後續通脹壓力。現貨層面，內外盤現貨仍寬鬆，倫敦市場白銀租賃利率低位執行。整體來看，白銀金融屬性偏空，工業屬性支撐有限，資金熱度亦偏弱。（中信期貨）

【中金：預計美聯儲10月仍可能暫停加息 12月再度行動】中金公司研報稱，美國9月CPI季調環比上漲0.6%，同比上漲3.7%；核心CPI環比上漲0.3%，同比上漲2.6%，均較上月反彈，且超出市場預期。漲價來源較廣，能源、核心商品及房租均形成推動。油價高企加劇能源通脹，消費韌性與AI投資需求支撐商品價格，租金粘性則制約服務通脹回落。貨幣政策方面，通脹反彈強化了美聯儲繼續加息的必要性，但由於近期美債收益率上行、美元走強，金融條件已部分收緊，美聯儲或保持耐心。中金預計10月仍可能暫停加息，12月再度行動。不過，加息節奏放緩並不意味著貨幣政策轉向寬鬆，未來一年，美聯儲或仍將維持偏緊的政策立場。

【摩根大通：並無“審慎理由”促使美國削減20年期國債發行】儘管市場“盛傳”美國財政部官員可能在週五公佈的一份調查問卷中，就削減20年期國債發行徵詢一級交易商意見，但摩根大通策略師認為，財政部“沒有審慎理由”這樣做。該行全球利率策略主管Jay Barry牽頭的團隊寫道，市場參與者“正迫不及待地等待11月季度再融資的一級交易商調查問卷，該問卷定於美國東部時間週五中午釋出”。“過去兩週，我們無數次被問到，財政部是否會藉此機會詢問削減甚至徹底停止發行20年期國債”。“出於多方面原因，我們認為探詢此類舉措並不審慎。”首先，“鑑於預算赤字仍然龐大，加之到期債務增加，財政部未來幾年將面臨顯著的融資缺口”。“其次，目前並不明視訊記憶體在長端市場失衡”。“最後，雖然這樣做可能暫時壓低長期收益率，但參考英國債務管理辦公室和日本財務省削減長端債券發行佔比的經驗，我們並不確定此舉能夠產生持久影響”。

### 2026-10-15 08:54:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Australian Shares Drop Further on Wall Street Jitters, Jobs Data — Australian stocks fell 42 points or 0.5% to 8,686 in early Thursday trading, extending losses to their weakest level in a week. A sharp Wall Street sell-off hit sentiment after hotter-than-expected U.S. inflation data rattled tech stocks and revived bets on Fed hikes. Locally, September jobs data showed the unemployment rate stuck at a near five-year high of 4.7% as employment unexpectedly declined. In major trading partner China, September CPI quickened but undershot forecasts, while producer prices rose more than expected; exports and imports surged to record highs, driving a strong trade surplus. Sectoral losses were led by tech, non-energy minerals, industrial services, and consumer durables, partly offset by gains in communications, consumer non-durables, and utilities. Heavyweight BHP Group slumped 3.8% despite securing a deal to avert a strike at Chile’s Escondida copper mine. Other major laggards included Goodman Group (-3.8%), Lynas Rare Earths (-3.7%), and Codan Ltd. (-3.4%).

Japan’s Nikkei Slumps on Hot US Inflation — The Nikkei 225 Index fell more than 3% toward the 65,000 level on Thursday, extending its losing streak to six consecutive sessions and reaching a two-week low as stronger-than-expected US inflation fueled concerns over higher interest rates. Japanese equities also followed an overnight technology-led selloff on Wall Street, as rising borrowing costs threatened the sector’s credit outlook amid substantial investments in artificial intelligence. AI-related stocks led the declines in Japan, with Kioxia Holdings falling 9.1%, Advantest dropping 7.4%, Lasertec losing 8.8%, SoftBank Group sliding 7.8% and Taiyo Yuden declining 4.5%. Banking stocks also weakened, including Mitsubishi UFJ and Sumitomo Mitsui, both down 1.1%, while Mizuho Financial fell 1.8%. Meanwhile, Japanese data showed that core machinery orders, a key indicator of capital investment, increased more than expected in August.

【Bolivia revises gold export rules to attract dollar inflows】Bolivia is overhauling gold export rules to boost USD inflows and dollar deposits at domestic banks, ending a three-year central bank-regulated certification regime. Under the new rules exporters must prove to mineral and metals trade regulator SENARECOM that at least 80% of export proceeds are deposited in Bolivian financial institutions; exporters will no longer need a central bank-issued certificate to ship gold. The reform, backed by the president and announced Wednesday, is aimed at increasing foreign-exchange inflows, improving USD availability and tightening oversight of gold exports.

### 2026-10-15 08:37:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Employment Change #Importance 1

Australia Employment Unexpectedly Falls — Employment in Australia dropped by 39,300 to 14.78 million in September 2026, reversing a downwardly revised 29,300 increase in the previous month and missing market expectations for a 20,000 gain. Part-time employment decreased by 73,700 to 4,550,200 people while full-time employment increased by 34,400 to 10,232,400. The employment-to-population ratio fell to 63.6% from 63.9% in August, and the participation rate eased to 66.8% from 67.0%. Compared with a year earlier, total employment expanded by 168,300, or 1.2%.

【海關總署：最佳化進境糧食企業監管措施及有關事宜】海關總署釋出《關於最佳化進境糧食企業監管措施及有關事宜的公告》。其中明確，進境糧食應當在具備防疫、處理等條件的指定加工企業進行加工使用。海關發現相關企業存在以下違規情形的，除依法追究相關企業責任外，要求其限期整改：（一）未按規定實施植物疫情監測及防除措施；（二）未按規定記錄進境糧食接卸、運輸、存放、加工、下腳料處理、發運流向等資訊；（三）進境糧食未按規定在指定企業加工、存放；（四）未按規定在進境糧食裝卸、運輸等環節採取防止撒漏、密封等防疫措施；（五）未按規定對進境糧食實施有效的除害處理或加工處理；（六）未按規定對下腳料、包裝材料實施無害化處理；（七）拒絕接受海關檢疫監督。如發現指定企業存在上述（四）（五）（六）（七）情形，在整改完成前，對後續擬運往該指定企業的檢疫審批申請，海關不予許可。

【US weighs easing sanctions on 'shadow fleet' to lower record-high shipping rates】An administration official said Wednesday the Trump administration is considering easing sanctions on so-called 'shadow fleet' vessels that previously carried oil and petroleum products from Russia, Iran and Venezuela, aiming to reduce global shipping rates, which remain at historic highs. The official said discussions focus on whether to allow previously sanctioned ships to transport 'legitimate oil'—crude or products not subject to U.S. sanctions, explicitly excluding sanctioned Russian or Iranian cargoes—to push more crude and refined flows out of the Gulf region. The move follows Trump’s recent temporary waiver of sanctions on Russian diesel, a decision criticized by Ukrainian President Zelenskiy, several European governments and some Republican senators.

US Futures Decline on Rate Hike Fears — US stock futures extended losses on Thursday as hotter-than-expected consumer inflation strengthened expectations of more aggressive Federal Reserve rate hikes. S&P 500 and Nasdaq 100 futures fell 0.3% and 0.9%, respectively, while Dow futures were flat. During Wednesday’s regular session, the S&P 500 and Nasdaq Composite declined 1.11% and 1.88%, respectively, with technology stocks leading the selloff amid growing concerns over AI-related investments. Data released Wednesday also showed that annual consumer price inflation accelerated to 3.7% in September, its highest level in four months and above market expectations of 3.6%. Investors are now awaiting Thursday’s producer inflation report, along with the latest retail sales data and weekly jobless claims figures. On the corporate front, earnings reports are expected from Charles Schwab, US Bancorp, Freeport-McMoRan, American Airlines, Snap, and other companies.

### 2026-10-15 08:08:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Machinery Orders #Importance 2

Japan Machinery Orders Rebound More than Expected — Japan’s core machine orders, which exclude volatile sectors such as ships and electric utilities, rose by 5.2% month-on-month to JPY 1,069.8 billion in August 2026, reversing a 3.7% decline in the previous month and exceeding market forecasts for a 3% gain. It marked the fourth month of increase so far this year, supported by rebounds in both manufacturing orders (1.6% vs -1.0% in July) to JPY 527.0 billion and non-manufacturing orders (2.9% vs -2.6%) to JPY 541.3 billion. Among manufacturers, non-ferrous metals posted the strongest growth (132.6%), followed by other transport equipment (43.7%), petroleum and coal products (19.6%), and automobiles, parts, and accessories (13.1%). In the non-manufacturing sector, goods leasing (136.0%), wholesale and retail trade (29.2%), and mining and quarrying posted solid rises. On an annual basis, machinery orders increased by 18.6%, up from 11.2% in July and above consensus of 15.7%. It was the fastest yearly growth in six months.

【長鑫科技釋出新型技術架構 海外龍頭壟斷壁壘面臨重估】國產儲存龍頭長鑫科技釋出新型技術架構，全球儲存龍頭股價近期明顯回撥。10月13日，三星電子、SK海力士跌幅超過7%，10月14日跌幅收窄企穩。業內人士向證券時報記者分析，國際儲存巨頭暴跌，短期是交易層面的情緒超調，長期來看反映了市場對其壟斷壁壘的重估，而未來伺服器市場會面臨分化。報道顯示，10月10日，長鑫科技總裁曹堪宇博士在第四屆整合晶片和芯粒大會上公佈公司在DRAM器件、4F^[2]架構及混合鍵合等方向進展，並預計年底推出融合新技術的新一代DDR5 RDIMM產品。相比DRAM行業主流6F^[2]架構，4F^[2]意味著每個單元僅佔4倍最小特徵尺寸的平方，因此單位面積內的電晶體數量會增多，實現儲存密度的提升。該技術最核心的特色是在DRAM儲存陣列電晶體上的創新，即自研的VSG（Vertical Surrounded Gate）電晶體。

【宜信系股權投資基金透視：3家機構管理規模逾170億元】宜信系複雜的股權投資，正逐步露出冰山一角。證券時報記者近日多方梳理發現，宜信系自2013年起即建立私募股權母基金投資團隊，主要透過3家股權機構在一級市場佈局。這3家股權機構分別為北京煜盈資產管理有限公司、北京瀚富資產管理有限公司和深圳普澤眾富資產管理有限公司。中國證券投資基金業協會備案資料顯示，該3家股權機構合計管理規模至少為170億元，根據未清算的管理基金註冊資本統計，規模超260億元。另據執中ZERONE資料，上述3家股權機構以母基金身份投資的子基金超過180只，合作的機構包括IDG資本、啟明創投、禮來亞洲基金、經緯創投等多家頭部普通合夥人（GP)。對這些母基金做股權穿透可見，其最終出資人以個人投資者為主。隨著宜信近日被立案偵查以及實控人唐寧、趙玫夫婦被採取刑事強制措施，這些龐大且複雜的股權投資基金如何清算，目前尚未明朗。

【證券日報：中國資產對國際資本吸引力持續提升】2026年以來，外資機構對中國資產加強調研、調高評級、加大持倉。這一趨勢背後，折射出國際資本對中國經濟韌性、產業升級成效與科技創新實力的高度認同。外資機構的調研節奏，是觀察其對A股公司興趣的直觀視窗。萬得資料顯示，截至10月14日，年內已有超650家外資機構累計調研A股公司6300餘次。其中，高盛（亞洲）證券以164次調研位居首位，Point72資產管理公司、美銀證券、摩根大通等外資機構的調研次數也均超過100次。 從調研行業分佈看，積體電路、機器視覺、工控自動化、電氣裝置等高階製造領域的上市公司更受外資機構關注。瀾起科技股份有限公司、‌奧比中光科技集團股份有限公司、深圳市匯川技術股份有限公司‌分別被81家、72家、57家外資機構列入自身關注度前三，新業務放量節奏、產線擴產與交付情況、下游業務應用場景等問題被重點關注。

【證券日報：汽車行業該擺脫銷量焦慮了】國內市場承壓既是消費需求變化的結果，也是產業走向成熟必須經歷的過程。中國汽車產業經歷多年快速擴張，正在進入由規模增長向質量提升轉變的關鍵階段。看待產業發展，不能簡單地以銷量增減判斷，更不必因短期資料波動而過度焦慮。評價汽車產業發展，不能只盯著銷量增長率，還要看技術進步、產品質量、企業盈利能力以及全球市場競爭力。銷量可以反映市場熱度，卻不是衡量產業實力的唯一標準。要防止銷量壓力演變為非理性競爭。能夠在市場波動中保持合理利潤和持續創新能力的企業，才真正具備長期競爭優勢。中國汽車產業已經走過依靠規模快速擴張的發展階段，接下來更需要的是有質量、有利潤、可持續的增長。銷量有起伏，市場有周期，都是產業發展的正常現象。真正值得警惕的不是增長速度暫時放緩，而是為了維持增速而透支產業未來。

The idea was floated at a Miami meeting last week attended by Trump’s peace envoy and officials from Ukraine, France, Germany, the UK, the EU and NATO, but European representatives rejected it. New Zealand Stocks Track Wall Street Lower — The NZX 50 lost 69 points, or 0.5%, to 13,691 in Thursday morning deals, after a slight gain in the previous session, tracking a decline on Wall Street overnight as faster-than-expected inflation data raised expectations of Fed rate hikes. Rising oil prices pressured sentiment amid concerns over inflation and the RBNZ's tightening monetary policy, after the central bank hiked interest rates in July and September. 【頂級預測師：美聯儲12月可能加息50個基點】Prestige Economics總裁Jason Schenker表示，FOMC可能會在12月考慮更大幅度、達50個基點的加息。這位較早預測到美聯儲最近一次加息的預測師Schenker如今認為，美聯儲可能在9月加息後“讓政策效果稍作喘息”，再於12月考慮更大幅度加息。Schenker預計，若12月出現更大幅度加息，明年年初可能暫停進一步加息。 在10月和12月會議之間，美聯儲官員還將收到兩份就業報告和另一份CPI資料，可為其未來決策提供依據。Schenker表示：“雖然只需等待6周，但他們將獲得更多資料。如果他們發現25個基點毫無作用，那麼我認為他們可能會推出50個基點的加息。”Schenker預計，更大幅度的加息可能導致明年年初暫停進一步加息；而如果12月僅加息25個基點，則明年第一季度再次加息的可能性較大。

US Crude Inventories Post Largest Draw Since January — US crude oil inventories fell by 11.12 million barrels in the week ending October 9th, far exceeding expectations for a 1.9 million-barrel increase and marking the largest draw since late January. US crude oil production rose to 13.98 million barrels per day in the week ending October 2nd, up from 13.95 million bpd a week earlier.

Arkansas's complaint alleges OpenAI's leadership acknowledged the risks and employees repeatedly raised serious safety concerns, yet the company accelerated releases of increasingly powerful models without sufficient testing or protections, prioritizing growth and profit. Joining Arkansas are Arizona, Iowa, Idaho, Kansas, Montana, Nebraska and West Virginia; all state attorneys general except Arizona are Republicans. The states seek court orders to force changes to OpenAI's safety operations, stronger protections for children and monetary damages.

【U.S. FMC proposes $200,000 fee for Spanish ships, import limits on olive oil】The U.S. Federal Maritime Commission (FMC) proposed measures to retaliate against Spain, including a $200,000 fee on Spanish-flagged vessels docking at U.S. ports and import restrictions on key Spanish exports such as olive oil. The FMC said Spain in 2024 refused berthing for three U.S. vessels on grounds their cargo was related to Israel’s war in Gaza; after an investigation the agency found Spain’s actions disadvantaged U.S.-flagged ships. ### 2026-10-15 04:36:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 1

Ibovespa Slips Amid Weak Economic Data — The Ibovespa edged down 0.2% to close at 206,506 on Wednesday in a volatile session amid weak economic data and an unfavorable external backdrop. Services activity contracted 0.1% in August, missing expectations for a 0.5% increase. Brazil’s Industrial Business Confidence Index rose 1.3 points to 46.2 in October but remained below the 50-point threshold, marking 22 consecutive months of pessimism. Oil prices extended their gains, stoking concerns over energy-driven inflation, while hotter-than-expected US CPI data pressured lenders amid increasing bets of a the Federal Reserve’s rate hike. Major banks declined, with Itaú down 1.4% and Bradesco losing 2.5%. Other financial stocks also fell, with B3 shedding 1.9% and Itaúsa losing 1.6%. Miners extended their losses, with Vale down 0.7% as Chinese iron ore prices continued to decline. In contrast, Petrobras rose 2.2% as oil prices resumed their climb. Investors awaited a new presidential runoff poll after the market close.

TSX Falls as Banks and Miners Slide — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell 1.1% to close at 35,584 on Wednesday, weighed down by losses in the banking and mining sectors. Oil prices extended their gains, stoking concerns over energy-driven inflation, while hotter-than-expected US CPI data pressured lenders amid increasing bets of a the Federal Reserve’s rate hike. Major banks declined, with BMO and National Bank both shedding 0.9%. Miners extended their losses as gold and silver prices continued to fall, with Agnico Eagle down 4.6%, Barrick losing 4% and WPM shedding 4.8%. In technology, Celestica plunged 9.2% as US chipmakers posted sharp losses, while Shopify fell 2.2%. In contrast, energy producers mostly advanced as oil prices resumed their climb, with Suncor up 1% and Imperial Oil adding 0.6%. Retailers also gained, with ATD rising 2.9% and Loblaw advancing 2.8%.

### 2026-10-15 04:19:34  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Rises on Middle East Tensions — Brent crude rose to around $106 a barrel on Wednesday amid renewed attacks across the Middle East and the IEA’s latest assessment of the oil market. The agency’s October Oil Market Report warned that constraints in diesel markets could persist well into 2027, with fresh attacks on Saudi Arabia threatening to derail a recovery in global oil supply. Fighting between Saudi Arabia and the Iran-backed Houthis continued to escalate, with Saudi Arabia’s Ministry of Energy reporting an overnight attack on an administrative building belonging to oil giant Saudi Aramco. The incident followed a series of Houthi attacks on Riyadh over the past week. A Saudi-led coalition reportedly struck Houthi energy terminals and fuel reserves in Yemen. Meanwhile, tensions between the US and Iran remained elevated. US Central Command rejected claims by the commander of Iran’s IRGC Navy that the Strait of Hormuz was under Iranian control and that any approaching US Navy warships would be “sunk.”.

US Stocks Dip Amid Hot Inflation and Chipmaker Weakness — US stock indices closed lower on Wednesday amid hotter-than-expected inflation data and continued volatility in the AI infrastructure sector. The S&P 500 fell 1.1%, the Nasdaq slipped 2%, and the Dow shed 49 points. Chipmakers extended this week’s losses after ASML said it could not keep pace with demand for semiconductor fabrication equipment, while Chinese competitors introduced improved products approaching the capabilities of US and South Korean firms. Meanwhile, headline and core inflation readings reinforced expectations that the Federal Reserve could deliver another rate hike this year. Nvidia lost 3.2%, Broadcom fell 3.1%, Micron declined 4.1% and AMD tumbled 5.7%. Among financial stocks, Bank of America fell 4.8% after both equity and fixed-income trading revenues missed estimates. Morgan Stanley closed 2.7% lower despite beating forecasts for equity-trading revenue, while BlackRock added 0.7% after stronger asset-management inflows boosted fee revenue.

【通脹火爆點燃10月加息預期 沃什曼谷講話或成關鍵風向標】美國9月通脹意外升溫，重新點燃了市場對美聯儲可能在本月加息的預期。此前數週，由於央行官員釋放出他們有時間等待的訊號，一度令本月加息看起來可能性不大。美聯儲主席沃什曾誓言不會以自己對近期貨幣政策前景的看法來引導市場。但這次通脹資料意外走高令外界更加關注紐約時間週四晚間他在曼谷的發言。屆時，他將與國際貨幣基金組織(IMF)總裁格奧爾基耶娃進行爐邊談話。由於議息會議即將到來，美聯儲將在本週六進入所謂的靜默期。這意味著如果央行要就本次會議可能作出的利率決定釋放訊號，或許只能由主席沃什來傳遞。美銀的美國利率策略師Meghan Swiber表示，投資者“等待沃什週四為美聯儲繼續加息開綠燈”。當談到通脹資料時，Swiber表示：“這次物價上漲的範圍相當廣，美聯儲難以對此置之不理。”

### 2026-10-15 04:04:55  #宏觀 #公司 #國際

【王毅會見俄羅斯外長拉夫羅夫】當地時間14日，中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅在巴庫會見俄羅斯外長拉夫羅夫。
王毅祝賀俄方成功舉行國家杜馬選舉，順利完成國內重要政治議程。王毅表示，今年5月和8月，習近平主席同普京總統舉行了富有成果的會晤，引領中俄新時代全面戰略協作夥伴關係持續高水平發展。下個月，亞太經合組織（APEC）領導人非正式會議將在深圳舉行。相信在俄方等各方支援下，APEC“中國年”將取得豐碩成果，攜手重振亞太共同體歷史程序，為亞太地區發展合作注入強勁動力。
王毅表示，亞歐大陸地域廣袤、文明多元，地區國家期盼維護和平穩定、深化多邊協作。亞信峰會時隔4年再次舉辦，中方願同包括俄方在內的亞信成員國密切協作，踐行真正的多邊主義，為完善全球治理貢獻力量。
拉夫羅夫表示，俄中在兩國元首戰略引領下，持續推進兩國關係和各領域合作，取得豐碩成果，符合兩國根本利益。俄方全力支援中方辦好APEC領導人非正式會議。俄中在國際事務上立場相近，俄方願同中方進一步增進在聯合國、二十國集團、APEC、金磚機制等多邊框架下的協作，共同維護國際公平正義。
雙方還就下一步中俄高層交往以及共同關心的國際地區熱點問題協調了各自立場，達成了新的共識。（新華社）

【Yemen government seeks US intelligence and training support, not seeking talks with Houthis】Yemen’s foreign minister Afraa Zuba said Wednesday the government has asked the United States for intelligence and training support for government forces and the coast guard and for continued political, economic and humanitarian assistance, without explicitly requesting US military aid. She urged the US to expand sanctions on the Houthi movement and to strengthen sanctions coordination with the EU. Zuba said the government is not seeking negotiations with the Houthis now, prioritizing military action to end Houthi control; she said such operations are decided, commanded and executed by Yemen, with coalition partners providing air support. ### 2026-10-15 03:12:13  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #S&P500 #Impact bearish

【Fed's GOOLSBEE: September inflation disappointing; more data needed before further hikes】Chicago Fed President GOOLSBEE said US September inflation surprised to the upside: core services inflation remains above 5% and core goods price gains have not eased as expected, implying inflation may be more persistent. He warned that if core inflation stays at a monthly annualized pace near 4% it would be wholly unacceptable and require policy action. GOOLSBEE said it is unclear whether current rates are sufficient to return inflation to the 2% target and he favors waiting for more data before deciding on further hikes, but cautioned that if inflation readings remain at current levels in coming months the Fed cannot stand pat. He highlighted a very tight labor market, strong growth, robust consumer spending and potential additional overheating from AI investment; firms in his district report little recession risk and widespread concern about rising costs and prices. If employment remains firm while inflation stays above target and moves in the wrong direction, the Fed must act to prevent further overheating.

### 2026-10-15 02:07:39  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #US Economy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Fed's Beige: High oil prices hit all 12 districts; firms expect further price rises】Fed's Beige reports overall U.S. growth but broadly weak momentum: among 12 Fed districts, two reported moderate growth, three slight, four modest and three flat. Manufacturing held up relatively well while consumer spending was little changed in most districts and, in over half, concentrated among higher-income households. On inf, three districts reported strong price gains, eight reported moderate gains and four said price increases accelerated from the prior period. All districts cited higher oil prices—elevated diesel and fuel surcharges are transmitting costs through supply chains—while tariffs, insurance and other commodity prices continue to pressure input costs. Some firms expect further price increases in coming months as existing contracts expire and hedges or delivery contracts are renegotiated. Employment was broadly unchanged but shortages of technical and skilled workers persist; five districts said some firms have reduced headcount via attrition, delayed hiring or expect fewer new roles following AI-driven productivity investments. Rising inf and benefits costs are pushing stronger wage demands. Most firms remain cautiously positive on the near-term outlook but expect limited growth and ongoing risks from energy prices, tariffs and other uncertainties.

【Trump: GlobalFoundries to build $21 bln NY fabs; Japan to fund; Korea to commit $50 bln to Alaska LNG】President Trump announced GlobalFoundries will invest $21 billion to build two large semiconductor fabs in Malta, New York, with funding provided by Japan under a prior trade agreement. The project is expected to create about 27,000 construction and supply‑chain jobs and more than 1,000 high‑skilled manufacturing roles. The administration said the U.S. government will take an equity stake in GlobalFoundries; the Commerce Department will add $955 million for silicon photonics, SiGe and advanced packaging, bringing total Commerce investment to $1.63 billion, and the government’s stake is said to exceed $2 billion. Trump also announced the launch of the Alaska LNG (AlaskaLNG) project, described as Alaska’s largest-ever energy infrastructure project; under an earlier trade and investment deal, South Korea will invest $50 billion. He said the project is now a federal government project, all required permits and authorizations are in place, and work can proceed immediately.

### 2026-10-15 00:24:34  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

CAC 40 Rises as Luxury Stocks Rebound — The CAC 40 rose 0.6% to close at 7,783 on Wednesday, rebounding from the previous session’s losses, supported by gains in luxury and industrial stocks. Luxury shares recovered after falling in the previous session amid weak results from LVMH, which cited softer Chinese demand. Hermes rose 0.9%, LVMH added 1.4%, and Kering gained 1.5%. The rebound followed Chinese foreign trade data showing that imports climbed 31% year-on-year to a record high in September, supported by seasonal restocking ahead of the major October Golden Week holiday. In the industrial sector, defense-related stocks led gains amid ongoing conflicts in the Middle East, with Airbus rising 2% and Thales gaining 1.8%. Meanwhile, ECB President Christine Lagarde played down speculation about intervention in France’s budget crisis, saying that solutions to the country’s precarious fiscal situation would need to come from domestic policymakers. Financial stocks closed lower.

European Stocks Close Higher — European equities closed higher on Wednesday, resisting sharp losses in North American equity markets amid positive corporate developments for local corporate giants. The Euro STOXX 50 added 0.5% to 6,195 and the STOXX Europe 600 advanced 0.5% to 638.0. ASML rose by 1.2% after it beat earnings and revenue expectations and issued a strong sales forecast for the fourth quarter. The Eurozone's largest company also said that AI-driven demand for its chipmaking equipment was so strong that it would be unable to fully meet customer demand in 2027 or 2028, adding that it was exploring ways to expand capacity. The move contrasted sharply with a pessimistic session for global AI infrastructure stocks amid the outlook of higher rates and improved offerings from Chinese competitors. Infineon dropped 1.2% and Siemens Energy sank 5.5%. Meanwhile, hot inflation from the US added bets of a Fed rate hike this year, pressuring banks. BNP Paribas, Intesa Sanpaolo, and Deutsche bank fell close to 1%.

FTSE MIB Edges Lower — The FTSE MIB closed 0.6% lower at 49,754 on Wednesday, pressured by heavyweight financials and tech stocks. Sovereign yields climbed after stronger-than-expected US inflation, driven by higher energy prices, fuelled uncertainty over a potential Fed rate hike in October. In Europe, ECB President Lagarde noted no signs of second-round effects from higher energy prices, while markets continue to expect a rate hike in December. AI-related stocks also declined as higher-for-longer rates threaten to raise borrowing costs for the sector’s already elevated AI buildout. Prysmian dropped 5.5%, while STMicroelectronics fell 1.8%. Utilities outperformed as Italy moved closer to an EU agreement on a mechanism to lower gas prices, with Italgas gaining 2.2% and A2A rising 1.2%. South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield Remains High — South Africa’s 10-year government bond yield hovered around 8.91%, staying near its highest since March, as geopolitical risks continued to cloud the outlook for inflation and interest rates. Oil prices extended their gains as escalating hostilities in the Middle East increased the risk of supply disruptions. In South Africa, the latest fuel price hikes are adding to concerns over inflation and growth, given the country's reliance on oil imports. Petrol and diesel prices climbed to unprecedented levels this month, surpassing the peak recorded in June and further complicating the central bank's goal to bring inflation back to 3%. The South African Reserve Bank (SARB) recently warned that the risk of second-round inflation effects had increased amid persistently elevated oil prices and uncertainty surrounding the impact of El Niño. Elsewhere, hotter-than-expected US inflation data prompted markets to reassess the Fed’s rate outlook, with a hike later this month becoming more likely.

### 2026-10-14 22:55:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Palladium Extends Slide to Over 1-Year Low — Palladium futures traded below $1,120 an ounce, extending their decline since September to a more than one-year low amid persistent investment outflows and a bearish long-term outlook. Stronger-than-expected US inflation pushed the odds of a Federal Reserve rate hike in October to nearly 50%, reversing earlier expectations that rates would remain unchanged until December. Higher-for-longer interest rates weigh on non-yielding assets such as palladium by increasing the opportunity cost of holding them. Over the longer term, the growing adoption of electric vehicles continues to pressure demand for palladium used in catalytic converters, although resilient demand for hybrids has helped moderate the decline. Despite this, the drop in demand continues to underpin a bearish outlook for the metal as it outpaces the contraction in supply, with elevated operating costs and power grid instability in South Africa, alongside ongoing trade risks in Russia, constraining production.

Platinum Falls Toward Three-Month Low — Platinum futures traded below $1,630 an ounce, approaching its lowest level in three months amid persistent investment outflows and a bearish long-term outlook. Stronger-than-expected US inflation pushed the odds of a Federal Reserve rate hike in October to nearly 50%, reversing earlier expectations that rates would remain unchanged until December. Higher-for-longer interest rates weigh on non-yielding assets such as platinum by increasing the opportunity cost of holding them. Over the longer term, the WPIC sees total demand falling 18%, including a 32% drop in Chinese jewelry demand and a 4% decline in automotive demand, although resilient demand for hybrids has helped moderate the decline. Despite this, the drop in demand continues to underpin a bearish outlook for the metal as it outpaces the contraction in supply, with elevated operating costs and power grid instability in South Africa, alongside ongoing trade risks in Russia, constraining production.

### 2026-10-14 22:51:53  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Currency #Importance 0

South African Rand Loses Ground — The South African rand weakened to around 16.6 per US dollar, nearing its July lows, as hotter-than-expected US inflation data for September boosted expectations of a Federal Reserve rate hike later this month, strengthening the dollar and weighing on emerging-market currencies. Lower prices for key precious metals, particularly gold and platinum-group metals (PGMs), added to the pressure by threatening export earnings. Meanwhile, escalating hostilities between Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi rebels pushed oil prices higher, raising import costs for South Africa, a net oil importer. The South African Reserve Bank recently warned that higher fuel and transport costs could spill over into broader prices, wages and inflation expectations, making inflationary pressures more persistent. This complicates the SARB's efforts to bring inflation back to its 3% target and adds uncertainty to the economic outlook ahead of National Treasury’s Medium-Term Budget Policy Statement on October 21.

### 2026-10-14 22:41:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Business Confidence #Importance 2

Brazil Industrial Confidence Improves in October — Brazil’s Industrial Business Confidence Index rose 1.3 points to 46.2 in October 2026 from September. Despite the improvement, the index remained below the 50-point threshold, marking 22 consecutive months of pessimism in the industrial sector. The increase was driven entirely by improved expectations, particularly regarding companies’ own business conditions. The Expectations Index climbed 2.1 points to 49.3, with the component measuring expectations for companies’ own business conditions rising 1.0 point to 52.3. Expectations for Brazil’s economy increased 4.3 points to 43.4. Meanwhile, the Current Conditions Index fell 0.2 point to 40.0, its lowest level since July 2020. The decline reflected a more negative assessment of companies’ own conditions, with that component dropping 0.5 point to 43.3. In contrast, the assessment of Brazil’s economic conditions rose 0.3 point to 33.3.

### 2026-10-14 22:38:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

Swiss Bond Yields Reach Two-Week High — The yield on the Swiss 10-year government bond rose to 0.57%, reaching its highest level in two weeks, tracking higher US Treasury yields following stronger-than-expected inflation that raised bets of a Fed rate hike in October. In Switzerland, inflation rose to a two-year high of 1% in September, mainly due to higher energy prices amid the prolonged Middle East conflict, but remained within the Swiss National Bank’s 0–2% target range. The SNB kept its policy rate at 0% in September, while Vice Chairman Martin said no rate adjustment is currently needed, citing low and stable inflation and limited spillover risks, as the Swiss economy was working "very well". Most economists expect the policy rate to remain unchanged through 2027, although markets continue to price in a rate hike by year-end and roughly three hikes by the end of 2027.

US Natgas Prices Fall from 3-Week High — US natural gas futures fell below $3.20 per million British thermal units, retreating from a nearly three-week high of $3.283 reached on October 13, mainly due to forecasts for relatively mild weather across the country through October 29, which are expected to limit both heating and cooling demand. Also, average feedgas deliveries to the nine major US liquefied natural gas export terminals eased to 17.5 billion cubic feet per day so far this month, down from 17.9 bcfd in September. On the other hand, domestic production in the Lower 48 states declined to 110.8 bcfd in October, compared with record averages of 113.3 bcfd in August and September. The reduction reflected hurricane-related Gulf of Mexico shut-ins, force majeure events and pipeline disruptions in several states. Meanwhile, analysts estimate inventories fell to 1.6% above seasonal norms in the week ending October 9, from 2% previously, ahead of Thursday’s government storage report.

Baltic Dry Index Down for 8th Day — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, continued to decline for the eighth consecutive session on Wednesday, slipping about 4.8% to its lowest since July 29 at 2,667 points. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, plunged 9.1% to its lowest level since late June at 3,630 points. Concurrently, the panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, dropped roughly 1% to 2,409 points. Among smaller vessels, the supramax index rose 0.7% to 1,845 points.

### 2026-10-14 22:32:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Stock Market #Importance 1

TSX Falls as Banks and Miners Slide — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell nearly 1% to trade below 36,000, weighed down by losses in the banking and mining sectors. Oil prices extended gains, fueling concerns over energy-driven inflation, while higher-than-expected US CPI inflation pressured lenders amid uncertainty over the Fed’s policy outlook. Major banks declined, with RBC, TD Bank, BMO and CIBC losing about 1% each. Miners extended their losses as gold and silver prices continued to fall, with Agnico Eagle down more than 4.5%, Barrick shedding over 3.5% and WPM losing nearly 4.5%. In contrast, energy producers gained as oil prices resumed their climb, with Suncor and Imperial Oil rising around 0.5%. On the data front, construction intentions in Canada rose 8.8% in August from July, well above expectations for a 2.9% increase. WSP Global edged higher, while AtkinsRéalis rose more than 0.5%. Technology stocks were mostly higher, with Shopify up 1%. Retailers also advanced, with ATD gaining 1.5% and Loblaw rising 1%.

### 2026-10-14 22:32:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #Italy #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

Italy's BTP Yield Rebounds — Italy’s 10-year BTP yield climbed to 4.58%, tracking higher US Treasury yields after higher-than-expected inflation data that raised bets of a Fed rate hike in October. In Europe, ECB President Largarde stated that no sign of second-round effects of high energy prices on inflation were seen yet, although caution remains of persistent inflationary pressures. As such, markets continue to expect a ECB hike in December. In Italy, its largest business lobby, Confindustria, has warned of a worsening energy shock. While its Italian economic growth forecasts for 2026 match the government's at 1%, growth for 2027 is seen lower at 0.6%, as the phaseout of free EU carbon permits could lead to loss of capacity and company closures. This adds to concerns over Italy's 2027 budget plan that's set to be above the EU's 3% of GDP limit, on optimistic growth assumptions. Italy’s debt-to-GDP ratio, currently at 138.6%, is also expected to surpass Greece’s this year as the eurozone’s most indebted country.

French Bond Yields Climb — France’s 10-year OAT yield rebounded above 4.85%, tracking higher US Treasury yields after stronger-than-expected US inflation data reinforced expectations of further Federal Reserve tightening. ECB President Christine Lagarde said energy-driven inflation has yet to spread across the wider euro area, supporting a wait-and-see approach. However, persistent price pressures and the ongoing US-Iran conflict have strengthened expectations of another ECB rate hike in December, following two increases earlier this year. French borrowing costs remain near their highest levels in over two decades, as record public debt and political hurdles complicate fiscal consolidation. The government aims to cut the budget deficit to 5% of GDP by 2027, but parliamentary support for significant spending cuts remains uncertain ahead of next year’s presidential election.

### 2026-10-14 21:52:33  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500

【TIMIRAOS: core inf above forecasts, bolstering case for another Fed hike this year】TIMIRAOS said September core CPI (ex-food and energy) rose 0.33% MoM, above nearly all Wall Street forecasts and up from 0.29% in August, marking a second consecutive month of stronger-than-expected inf. He said the report reinforces the case for the Federal Reserve to raise rates again this year; Fed officials unanimously voted to hike last month and most had expected one more increase in the year. The release raises two policy questions: whether officials can wait until December to act after saying they do not expect to change rates at the meeting in two weeks, and how high policy rates ultimately need to go — a judgement that depends on whether rising energy costs and AI-driven investment pressures spread into the broader economy. Officials have not signalled they would revise plans based on a single report.

### 2026-10-14 21:42:37  #資料 #市場 #央行 #國際

【美聯儲傳聲筒 Nick Timiraos：CPI超預期令美聯儲10月按兵不動面臨更大壓力，但尚無跡象表明官員將改變利率決策計劃】美聯儲傳聲筒，名記Nick Timiraos釋出文章指出，美國9月核心通脹連續第二個月超出預期，進一步強化了美聯儲年內再加息一次的理由。美聯儲官員上月一致投票決定加息時，幾乎所有官員都在利率預測中預計今年還將加息一次。剔除食品和能源價格的美國9月核心消費者價格指數（CPI）環比上漲0.33%，高於華爾街經濟學家幾乎所有預測值，8月漲幅為0.29%。
這份通脹報告給美聯儲官員帶來了兩個問題。
首先，更迫切的問題是，美聯儲是否仍有餘地等到12月再加息。此前，美聯儲官員釋放訊號，表示預計不會在兩週後的政策會議上調整利率。一些分析人士認為，如果美聯儲官員本就預計需要進一步加息，那麼沒有太多理由繼續等待。不過，目前尚無跡象表明，官員們會因為單獨一份通脹報告而重新考慮近期的政策安排。
其次，也是更重要的問題，是利率最終需要上升到多高。這將取決於能源價格上漲和人工智慧投資熱潮帶來的通脹壓力，是否正在向更廣泛的經濟領域蔓延。

UK Gilt Yields Near 2007 Highs — The UK 10-year gilt yield rose to 5.46%, nearing its highest level since July 2007, tracking higher US Treasury yields after stronger-than-expected US inflation boosted expectations of a Federal Reserve rate hike this month. Meanwhile, BoE Chief Economist Huw Pill expressed skepticism about market expectations for four rate increases over the coming year, arguing that stronger evidence was needed to justify a larger hike despite the need to address the energy shock triggered by the Iran war. Governor Andrew Bailey, however, warned that a rate increase would become increasingly difficult to avoid if the energy shock persists. Markets see a near-100% chance of a BoE rate hike in November, its first in over three years, though expectations for four increases by June 2027 have eased slightly. Separately, the IMF urged the UK government to clarify how it will fund higher defense spending, with an additional £11 billion annually needed to raise military expenditure to 3% of GDP by 2030.

### 2026-10-14 21:30:12  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Currency #Importance 0

Swiss Franc Weakens to 17-Month Low — The Swiss franc weakened toward 0.84 per USD, reaching its lowest level in seventeen months following stronger-than-expected US inflation data that raised bets of a rate hike by the Federal Reserve in October and boosted demand for the greenback. In contrast, the Swiss National Bank is expected to leave its policy rate unchanged at 0%, while its Vice Chairman Martin said no adjustment was currently needed, citing low and stable inflation and limited spillover risks, as the Swiss economy was working "very well". Policymakers have also scaled back threats of currency intervention. Swiss borrowing costs have remained at the world’s lowest for over a year, increasing it’s appeal as a funding source for carry trades as interest-rate differentials with other major economies widen. As traders take short positions, the selling pressure of franc-loans weakens the currency.

### 2026-10-14 21:24:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

Bund Yields Rebound After Strong US Inflation — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield climbed to 3.49%, tracking higher US Treasury yields after stronger-than-expected US inflation data showed consumer prices and core inflation rising more than anticipated in September, increasing the likelihood of a Federal Reserve rate hike this month. Markets now see around a 50% chance of a hike, up from roughly 22% before the report. Meanwhile, ECB President Christine Lagarde said elevated energy-driven inflation has yet to spread across the broader euro-area economy, supporting a wait-and-see approach. However, persistent inflation surprises and the ongoing US-Iran conflict have prompted markets to price in another ECB rate hike in December. Bond markets have shown tentative signs of stabilization following a global sell-off in late September and early October, but persistent concerns over energy prices, inflation and rising borrowing costs, alongside fiscal pressures in more heavily indebted countries, continue to weigh on sentiment.

### 2026-10-14 21:15:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Currency #Importance 1

Sterling Falls to Late-June Lows — Sterling fell below $1.32, returning to levels last seen in late June, as stronger-than-expected US inflation reduced expectations of the Federal Reserve holding rates steady in October. US inflation rose to 3.7% year-on-year in September, above forecasts of 3.6%, while core inflation also exceeded expectations. Meanwhile, investors assessed comments from Bank of England policymakers, with Chief Economist Huw Pill expressing skepticism about market expectations for four rate hikes over the coming year and arguing that stronger evidence was needed to justify a larger increase despite the need to address the energy shock triggered by the Iran war. Governor Andrew Bailey, however, warned that a rate hike would become harder to avoid the longer the shock persists. Markets are pricing in a near-100% chance of the BoE delivering its first rate hike in more than three years in November. However, expectations for four 25-basis-point increases by June 2027 have eased slightly.

### 2026-10-14 21:11:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Currency #Importance 1

Euro Hits 16-Month Low — The euro tumbled to $1.11, touching its weakest level since May 2025, as investors flocked to the US dollar following stronger-than-expected inflation data that reduced the likelihood of the Federal Reserve holding rates steady in October. US inflation rose to 3.7% year-on-year in September, above forecasts of 3.6%, while core inflation also exceeded expectations. Markets now see around a 50% chance of the Fed keeping rates unchanged this month, down from over 78% before the report. Meanwhile, expectations that the ECB will delay further rate hikes until December, alongside concerns over the impact of rising inflation and borrowing costs on economic growth, continued to weigh on the common currency. Fiscal pressures in highly indebted countries, particularly France, added to the downside risks. ECB President Christine Lagarde noted that elevated energy-driven inflation has yet to spread across the broader euro-area economy, reinforcing the case for a wait-and-see approach.

【UN warns US‑Iran impasse on Hormuz fertilizer plan could trigger food‑security shock in Asia, Africa】Refinitiv reports a UN official leading a UN fertilizer‑shipping initiative warned that if the US and Iran do not agree within weeks on a UN‑backed plan to move fertilizer through the Strait of Hormuz, parts of Asia and Africa could face a food‑security crisis. The UN created a working group in March and has drafted a mechanism that remains unimplemented. The proposal would set up a UN coordination center in Oman to register vessels and coordinate safe transit; UN monitors would verify cargoes with Gulf port authorities. The UN deputy secretary‑general said five vessels per day would be sufficient to sustain supplies to needy countries; vessels would be tracked in transit and subject to additional inspections if concerns arise.

### 2026-10-14 20:57:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Falls Below $58 — Silver prices fell below $58 an ounce on Wednesday, hitting their lowest level since early August, as stronger-than-expected US inflation prompted investors to reassess the Federal Reserve’s interest-rate outlook. US inflation climbed to 3.7% year-on-year in September, above forecasts of 3.6%, driven by higher energy prices. Monthly CPI rose 0.6%, its largest increase in five months, while core inflation also exceeded expectations, reaching 2.6% annually and 0.3% monthly. According to LSEG data, markets now see around a 50% chance of the Fed holding rates steady in October, down from over 78% before the report. Meanwhile, the probability of a rate hike in December has risen to nearly 99%, from 88% previously. Although recent Fed communications had offered little indication of consecutive rate hikes, the stronger-than-expected core CPI reading has brought an October move back into consideration, weighing on non-yielding precious metals such as silver.

Gold Slides as Strong US Inflation Fuels Rate Hike Bets — Gold prices fell below $4,100 an ounce on Wednesday, hitting their lowest level since early August, as a stronger-than-expected US inflation report prompted markets to reassess the Federal Reserve’s rate outlook. US inflation rose to 3.7% year-on-year in September, above forecasts of 3.6%, driven by higher energy prices. Monthly CPI increased 0.6%, its biggest gain in five months, while core inflation also exceeded expectations, reaching 2.6% annually and 0.3% monthly. Meanwhile, the probability of a rate hike in December has risen to nearly 99%, from 88% previously. US Equity Futures Swing Lower After CPI — US stock futures swung lower on Wednesday amid hot inflation data and continued volatility in the AI infrastructure sector. Contracts for the S&P 500 and Dow fell 0.3%, while those for the Nasdaq 100 slipped nearly 1%. Chip producers extended this week's losses as ASML stated it could not keep up with fab demand, while Chinese competitors introduced improved products approaching the capabilities of US and Korean firms. Meanwhile, headline and core inflation prints solidified expectations that the Federal Reserve will deliver another rate hike this year. AMD and Intel fell 2%, while Dell, SanDisk, and Palo Alto Networks sank over 3% premarket. ### 2026-10-14 20:50:04  #MKT News #Star Stocks #NVIDIA #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #Nasdaq100 #Impact bullish #S&P500 #Nikkei 225

【Japan to fund up to $21 bln for GlobalFoundries US fabs; US to take roughly 4% stake】GlobalFoundries will build two fabs on its existing site in upstate New York with up to $21 billion of Japanese financing, according to an agreement announced Wednesday. The plants will be jointly held by the United States and Japan, and the U.S. government will acquire about a 4% stake in GlobalFoundries. Construction funding will come from Japan’s previously pledged $550 billion of investment in the U.S. The new facilities will produce foundational chips—lower-complexity but widely used semiconductors for autos, industrial equipment and communications—to boost U.S. supply and reduce reliance on East Asian vendors. The deal is pitched as a response to U.S. national-security concerns and to ease base-chip tightness intensified as industry capacity shifts toward advanced AI accelerators such as NVIDIA’s.

### 2026-10-14 20:49:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Building Permits MoM #Importance 1

Canada Building Permits Rebound in August — Canada’s building permits rose 8.8% month-on-month to C$13.6 billion in August 2026, rebounding from a revised 15.8% decline in July and exceeding forecasts for a 2.6% increase. The recovery was led by the residential sector, where permit values rose C$1.1 billion to C$8.5 billion. The increase was driven by the multi-unit component, which climbed C$1.1 billion to C$5.8 billion, while the single-family component edged down C$0.7 million to C$2.6 billion. Meanwhile, the value of non-residential building permits fell C$29.8 million to C$5.1 billion. A C$380.0 million decline in the commercial component, to C$2.2 billion, was largely offset by gains in the industrial component, which rose C$317.3 million to C$1.1 billion, and the institutional component, which increased C$32.9 million to C$1.8 billion. Overall, building permits rose 12.4% year-on-year.

Treasury Yields Jump After Hot CPI Report — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note surged 9bps to 5.3% on Wednesday, approaching again highs last seen in early 2002, after the latest US inflation report came in hotter than expected. Annual inflation accelerated to 3.7%, above forecasts of 3.6%, while the annual core inflation rate edged up to 2.6%, exceeding expectations of 2.5%. DXY Hits 15-Month High — The US dollar climbed as much as 0.5% to 102.72 on Wednesday, reaching its highest level since April 2025 after fresh inflation figures strengthened expectations of further Federal Reserve tightening. Consumer prices increased 0.6% in September from the previous month, the fastest rise in five months, while annual inflation reached 3.7%. Although the headline figures were broadly in line with forecasts, underlying inflation proved more persistent than expected. Core CPI, which excludes food and energy, rose 0.3% monthly and 2.6% annually, reinforcing concerns that elevated energy costs are keeping price pressures high. At the same time, Brent crude continued to rise on escalating Middle East tensions and their potential impact on supply. However, Fed Vice Chair for Supervision Michelle Bowman said she saw no immediate need for another increase following last month’s hike, despite inflation remaining above target.

US CPI Rises 0.6% in September as Energy Prices Surge — US consumer prices rose 0.6% month-on-month in September 2026, accelerating from 0.4% in August, in line with expectations and marking the largest monthly increase since April. The energy index surged 5.2%, following a 2.1% rise in August, driven largely by a 9.1% jump in gasoline prices, which accounted for more than half of the overall CPI increase. Shelter costs rose 0.4%, up from 0.3% in August, while food prices were unchanged as higher dining-out costs offset falling grocery prices. Meanwhile, core CPI, excluding food and energy, increased 0.3%, exceeding expectations for a 0.2% rise.

US Core CPI Rises More than Expected — Core consumer prices in the United States, which exclude food and energy, rose by 0.3% from the previous month in September of 2026, the same pace as August, and ahead of median market expectations of a 0.2% increase. Consumer prices rose sharply for shelter (0.4% vs 0.3% in August) and transportation services (0.6% vs 0.5%), the most in six months to suggest that soaring energy prices are being transmitted through the transportation and logistics sector. Meanwhile, the CPI rebounded for medical care services (0.3% vs -0.2%). From the previous year, the core inflation rate was at 2.6%.

### 2026-10-14 20:31:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Inflation Rate #Importance 3

US Inflation Rate Tops Forecasts — The US annual inflation rate accelerated to 3.7% in September 2026, up from 3.4% in each of the previous two months and above market expectations of 3.6%, driven by higher energy prices. Gasoline rose 34.2% yoy, more than 27.4% in August and fuel oil prices increased 74.2%, compared with 52% previously. Inflation also accelerated for shelter, to 3.2% from 3%, but slowed for food to 2.5% from 2.7%. On a monthly basis, the CPI went up 0.6%, marking its largest gain in five months, compared to 0.4% in August and in line with forecasts. Gasoline jumped 9.1%, accounting for over half of the monthly all items increase. Recreation, used cars and trucks, education, airline fares, medical care, and apparel also went up while food prices steadied and communication declined. Meanwhile, annual core inflation rate went up to 2.6% from 2.4%, more than forecasts of 2.5% while monthly core CPI inflation steadied at 0.3%, the same as in August and defying forecasts it would slow to 0.2%.
